const model = `// Code generated by 'yaegi extract {{.ImportPath}}'. DO NOT EDIT.

{{.License}}

{{if .BuildTags}}// +build {{.BuildTags}}{{end}}

package {{.Dest}}

import (
{{- range $key, $value := .Imports }}
	{{- if $value}}
	"{{$key}}"
	{{- end}}
{{- end}}
{{- if or .Val .Typ }}
	"{{.ImportPath}}"
{{- end}}
	"reflect"
)

func init() {
	Symbols["{{.PkgName}}"] = map[string]reflect.Value{
		{{- if .Val}}
		// function, constant and variable definitions
		{{range $key, $value := .Val -}}
			{{- if $value.Addr -}}
				"{{$key}}": reflect.ValueOf(&{{$value.Name}}).Elem(),
			{{else -}}
				"{{$key}}": reflect.ValueOf({{$value.Name}}),
			{{end -}}
		{{end}}

		{{- end}}
		{{- if .Typ}}
		// type definitions
		{{range $key, $value := .Typ -}}
			"{{$key}}": reflect.ValueOf((*{{$value}})(nil)),
		{{end}}

		{{- end}}
		{{- if .Wrap}}
		// interface wrapper definitions
		{{range $key, $value := .Wrap -}}
			"_{{$key}}": reflect.ValueOf((*{{$value.Name}})(nil)),
		{{end}}
		{{- end}}
	}
}
{{range $key, $value := .Wrap -}}
	// {{$value.Name}} is an interface wrapper for {{$key}} type
	type {{$value.Name}} struct {
		IValue interface{}
		{{range $m := $value.Method -}}
		W{{$m.Name}} func{{$m.Param}} {{$m.Result}}
		{{end}}
	}
	{{range $m := $value.Method -}}
		func (W {{$value.Name}}) {{$m.Name}}{{$m.Param}} {{$m.Result}} {
			{{- if eq $m.Name "String"}}
			if W.WString == nil {
				return ""
			}
			{{end}}
			{{- $m.Ret}} W.W{{$m.Name}}{{$m.Arg -}}
		}
	{{end}}
{{end}}
`

// Val stores the value name and addressable status of symbols.
type Val struct {
	Name string // "package.name"
	Addr bool   // true if symbol is a Var
}

// Method stores information for generating interface wrapper method.
type Method struct {
	Name, Param, Result, Arg, Ret string
}

// Wrap stores information for generating interface wrapper.
type Wrap struct {
	Name   string
	Method []Method
}

// restricted map defines symbols for which a special implementation is provided.
var restricted = map[string]bool{
	"osExit":        true,
	"osFindProcess": true,
	"logFatal":      true,
	"logFatalf":     true,
	"logFatalln":    true,

// Package stdlib provides wrappers of standard library packages to be imported natively in Yaegi.
package stdlib

import "reflect"

// Symbols variable stores the map of stdlib symbols per package.
var Symbols = map[string]map[string]reflect.Value{}

// MapTypes variable contains a map of functions which have an interface{} as parameter but
// do something special if the parameter implements a given interface.
var MapTypes = map[reflect.Value][]reflect.Type{}

	Symbols["github.com/traefik/yaegi/stdlib/stdlib"] = map[string]reflect.Value{
		"Symbols": reflect.ValueOf(Symbols),
	}
	Symbols["."] = map[string]reflect.Value{

	}
}

// getPrompt returns a function which prints a prompt only if input is a terminal.
func getPrompt(in io.Reader, out io.Writer) func(reflect.Value) {
	forcePrompt, _ := strconv.ParseBool(os.Getenv("YAEGI_PROMPT"))
	if forcePrompt {
		return doPrompt(out)
	}
	s, ok := in.(interface{ Stat() (os.FileInfo, error) })
	if !ok {
		return func(reflect.Value) {}
	}
	stat, err := s.Stat()
	if err == nil && stat.Mode()&os.ModeCharDevice != 0 {
		return doPrompt(out)
	}
	return func(reflect.Value) {}
}

// Use loads binary runtime symbols in the interpreter context so
// they can be used in interpreted code.
func (interp *Interpreter) Use(values Exports) error {
	for k, v := range values {
		importPath := path.Dir(k)
		packageName := path.Base(k)

		if k == "." && v["MapTypes"].IsValid() {
			// Use mapping for special interface wrappers.
			for kk, vv := range v["MapTypes"].Interface().(map[reflect.Value][]reflect.Type) {
				interp.mapTypes[kk] = vv
			}
			continue
		}

		if importPath == "." {
			return fmt.Errorf("export path %[1]q is missing a package name; did you mean '%[1]s/%[1]s'?", k)
		}

		if importPath == selfPrefix {
			interp.hooks.Parse(v)
			continue
		}

		if interp.binPkg[importPath] == nil {
			interp.binPkg[importPath] = make(map[string]reflect.Value)
			interp.pkgNames[importPath] = packageName
		}

		for s, sym := range v {
			interp.binPkg[importPath][s] = sym
		}
		if k == selfPath {
			interp.binPkg[importPath]["Self"] = reflect.ValueOf(interp)
		}
	}

	// Checks if input values correspond to stdlib packages by looking for one
	// well known stdlib package path.
	if _, ok := values["fmt/fmt"]; ok {
		fixStdlib(interp)

		// Load stdlib generic source.
		for _, s := range gen.Sources {
			if _, err := interp.Compile(s); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// fixStdlib redefines interpreter stdlib symbols to use the standard input,
// output and errror assigned to the interpreter. The changes are limited to
// the interpreter only.
// Note that it is possible to escape the virtualized stdio by
// read/write directly to file descriptors 0, 1, 2.
func fixStdlib(interp *Interpreter) {

END STEP

// Copyright 2023 The Go Authors. All rights reserved.
// Use of this source code is governed by a BSD-style
// license that can be found in the LICENSE file.

// Package cmp provides types and functions related to comparing
// ordered values.
package cmp

// Ordered is a constraint that permits any ordered type: any type
// that supports the operators < <= >= >.
// If future releases of Go add new ordered types,
// this constraint will be modified to include them.
//
// Note that floating-point types may contain NaN ("not-a-number") values.
// An operator such as == or < will always report false when
// comparing a NaN value with any other value, NaN or not.
// See the [Compare] function for a consistent way to compare NaN values.
type Ordered interface {
	~int | ~int8 | ~int16 | ~int32 | ~int64 |
		~uint | ~uint8 | ~uint16 | ~uint32 | ~uint64 | ~uintptr |
		~float32 | ~float64 |
		~string
}

// Less reports whether x is less than y.
// For floating-point types, a NaN is considered less than any non-NaN,
// and -0.0 is not less than (is equal to) 0.0.
func Less[T Ordered](x, y T) bool {
	return (isNaN(x) && !isNaN(y)) || x < y
}

// Compare returns
//
//	-1 if x is less than y,
//	 0 if x equals y,
//	+1 if x is greater than y.
//
// For floating-point types, a NaN is considered less than any non-NaN,
// a NaN is considered equal to a NaN, and -0.0 is equal to 0.0.
func Compare[T Ordered](x, y T) int {
---
// Copyright 2021 The Go Authors. All rights reserved.
// Use of this source code is governed by a BSD-style
// license that can be found in the LICENSE file.

// Package maps defines various functions useful with maps of any type.
package maps

// Equal reports whether two maps contain the same key/value pairs.
// Values are compared using ==.
func Equal[M1, M2 ~map[K]V, K, V comparable](m1 M1, m2 M2) bool {
	if len(m1) != len(m2) {
		return false
	}
	for k, v1 := range m1 {
		if v2, ok := m2[k]; !ok || v1 != v2 {
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}

returncode=0
					}
				}
			case ident == "init":
				// init functions do not get declared as per the Go spec.
			default:
				asImportName := path.Join(ident, baseName)
				if _, exists := sc.sym[asImportName]; exists {
					// redeclaration error
					err = n.cfgErrorf("%s redeclared in this block", ident)
					return false
				}
				// Add a function symbol in the package name space except for init
				sc.sym[ident] = &symbol{kind: funcSym, typ: n.typ, node: n, index: -1}
			}
			if !n.typ.isComplete() && !genericMethod {
				revisit = append(revisit, n)
			}
			return false

		case importSpec:
			var name, ipath string
			if len(n.child) == 2 {
				ipath = constToString(n.child[1].rval)
				name = n.child[0].ident
			} else {
				ipath = constToString(n.child[0].rval)
			}
			// Try to import a binary package first, or a source package
			var pkgName string
			if packageName := path.Base(ipath); path.Dir(ipath) == packageName {
				ipath = packageName
			}
			if pkg := interp.binPkg[ipath]; pkg != nil {
				switch name {
				case "_": // no import of symbols
				case ".": // import symbols in current scope
					for n, v := range pkg {
						typ := v.Type()
						kind := binSym
						if isBinType(v) {
							typ = typ.Elem()
							kind = typeSym
						}
						sc.sym[n] = &symbol{kind: kind, typ: valueTOf(typ, withScope(sc)), rval: v}
					}
				default: // import symbols in package namespace
					if name == "" {
						name = interp.pkgNames[ipath]
					}

					// If an incomplete type exists, delete it
					if sym, exists := sc.sym[name]; exists && sym.kind == typeSym && sym.typ.incomplete {
						delete(sc.sym, name)
					}

					// Imports of a same package are all mapped in the same scope, so we cannot just
					// map them by their names, otherwise we could have collisions from same-name
					// imports in different source files of the same package. Therefore, we suffix
					// the key with the basename of the source file.
					name = path.Join(name, baseName)
					if sym, exists := sc.sym[name]; !exists {
						sc.sym[name] = &symbol{kind: pkgSym, typ: &itype{cat: binPkgT, path: ipath, scope: sc}}
						break
					} else if sym.kind == pkgSym && sym.typ.cat == srcPkgT && sym.typ.path == ipath {
						// ignore re-import of identical package
						break
					}

					// redeclaration error. Not caught by the parser.
					err = n.cfgErrorf("%s redeclared in this block", name)
					return false
				// TODO(mpl): move any of that code to typecheck?
				c.typ.node = c
				if !c.typ.assignableTo(typ) {
					err = c.cfgErrorf("cannot use %v (type %v) as type %v in return argument", c.ident, c.typ.cat, typ.cat)
					return
				}
				if c.typ.cat == nilT {
					// nil: Set node value to zero of return type
					c.rval = reflect.New(typ.TypeOf()).Elem()
				}
			}

		case selectorExpr:
			wireChild(n)
			n.typ = n.child[0].typ
			n.recv = n.child[0].recv
			if n.typ == nil {
				err = n.cfgErrorf("undefined type")
				break
			}
			switch {
			case n.typ.cat == binPkgT:
				// Resolve binary package symbol: a type or a value
				name := n.child[1].ident
				pkg := n.child[0].sym.typ.path
				if s, ok := interp.binPkg[pkg][name]; ok {
					if isBinType(s) {
						n.typ = valueTOf(s.Type().Elem())
					} else {
						n.typ = valueTOf(fixPossibleConstType(s.Type()), withUntyped(isValueUntyped(s)))
						n.rval = s
						if pkg == "unsafe" && (name == "AlignOf" || name == "Offsetof" || name == "Sizeof") {
							n.sym = &symbol{kind: bltnSym, node: n, rval: s}
							n.ident = pkg + "." + name
						}
					}
					n.action = aGetSym
					n.gen = nop
				} else {
					err = n.cfgErrorf("package %s \"%s\" has no symbol %s", n.child[0].ident, pkg, name)
				}

END STEP

package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"go/ast"
	"go/parser"
	"go/token"
)

func main() {
	src := `package main

import _ "embed"

//go:embed hello.txt
var s string

//go:embed a.txt
//go:embed b.txt
var (
	x string
	//go:embed c.txt
	y string
)

// note comment
//go:embed z.txt
var z string
`
	fset := token.NewFileSet()
	f, _ := parser.ParseFile(fset, "x.go", src, parser.ParseComments)
	for _, d := range f.Decls {
		gd, ok := d.(*ast.GenDecl)
		if !ok || gd.Tok != token.VAR { continue }
		fmt.Println("GenDecl:")
		if gd.Doc != nil { printCG("  Doc", gd.Doc) }
		for _, s := range gd.Specs {
			vs := s.(*ast.ValueSpec)
			fmt.Printf("  ValueSpec %v:\n", vs.Names[0].Name)
			if vs.Doc != nil { printCG("    Doc", vs.Doc) }
		}
	}
}

func printCG(label string, cg *ast.CommentGroup) {
	fmt.Printf("%s: Text=%q\n", label, cg.Text())
	for _, c := range cg.List {
		fmt.Printf("    %q at %v\n", c.Text, c.Slash)
	}
}

// interpreter's FileSet.
func (interp *Interpreter) ast(f ast.Node) (string, *node, error) {
	var err error
	var root *node
	var anc astNode
	var st nodestack
	pkgName := "main"

	addChild := func(root **node, anc astNode, pos token.Pos, kind nkind, act action) *node {
		var i interface{}
		nindex := atomic.AddInt64(&interp.nindex, 1)
		n := &node{anc: anc.node, interp: interp, index: nindex, pos: pos, kind: kind, action: act, val: &i, gen: builtin[act]}
		n.start = n
		if anc.node == nil {
			*root = n
		} else {
			anc.node.child = append(anc.node.child, n)
			if anc.node.action == aCase {
				ancAst := anc.ast.(*ast.CaseClause)
				if len(ancAst.List)+len(ancAst.Body) == len(anc.node.child) {
					// All case clause children are collected.
					// Split children in condition and body nodes to desambiguify the AST.
					nindex = atomic.AddInt64(&interp.nindex, 1)
					body := &node{anc: anc.node, interp: interp, index: nindex, pos: pos, kind: caseBody, action: aNop, val: &i, gen: nop}

					if ts := anc.node.anc.anc; ts.kind == typeSwitch && ts.child[1].action == aAssign {
						// In type switch clause, if a switch guard is assigned, duplicate the switch guard symbol
						// in each clause body, so a different guard type can be set in each clause
						name := ts.child[1].child[0].ident
						nindex = atomic.AddInt64(&interp.nindex, 1)
						gn := &node{anc: body, interp: interp, ident: name, index: nindex, pos: pos, kind: identExpr, action: aNop, val: &i, gen: nop}
						body.child = append(body.child, gn)
					}

					// Add regular body children
					body.child = append(body.child, anc.node.child[len(ancAst.List):]...)
					for i := range body.child {
						body.child[i].anc = body
					}
					anc.node.child = append(anc.node.child[:len(ancAst.List)], body)
				}
			}
		}
		return n
	}

	// Populate our own private AST from Go parser AST.
	// A stack of ancestor nodes is used to keep track of current ancestor for each depth level
	ast.Inspect(f, func(nod ast.Node) bool {
		anc = st.top()
		var pos token.Pos
		if nod != nil {
			pos = nod.Pos()
		}
		switch a := nod.(type) {
		case nil:
			anc = st.pop()

		case *ast.ArrayType:
			st.push(addChild(&root, anc, pos, arrayType, aNop), nod)

		case *ast.AssignStmt:
			var act action
			var kind nkind
			if len(a.Lhs) > 1 && len(a.Rhs) == 1 {
				if a.Tok == token.DEFINE {
					kind = defineXStmt
				} else {
					kind = assignXStmt
				}
				act = aAssignX
			} else {
				kind = assignStmt
				switch a.Tok {
				case token.ASSIGN:
					act = aAssign
				case token.ADD_ASSIGN:
					act = aAddAssign
				case token.AND_ASSIGN:
					act = aAndAssign
				case token.AND_NOT_ASSIGN:
					act = aAndNotAssign
				case token.DEFINE:
					kind = defineStmt
					act = aAssign
				case token.SHL_ASSIGN:
					act = aShlAssign
				case token.SHR_ASSIGN:
					act = aShrAssign
				case token.MUL_ASSIGN:
					act = aMulAssign
				case token.OR_ASSIGN:
					act = aOrAssign
				case token.QUO_ASSIGN:
					act = aQuoAssign
				case token.REM_ASSIGN:
					act = aRemAssign
				case token.SUB_ASSIGN:
					act = aSubAssign
				case token.XOR_ASSIGN:
					act = aXorAssign
				}
			}
			n := addChild(&root, anc, pos, kind, act)
			n.nleft = len(a.Lhs)
			n.nright = len(a.Rhs)
			st.push(n, nod)

		case *ast.BasicLit:
			n := addChild(&root, anc, pos, basicLit, aNop)
			n.ident = a.Value

END STEP

				kind = forStmt2
			case a.Cond != nil && a.Init != nil && a.Post == nil:
				kind = forStmt3
			case a.Cond == nil && a.Init == nil && a.Post != nil:
				kind = forStmt4

type astNode struct {
	node *node
	ast  ast.Node
}

type nodestack []astNode

func (s *nodestack) push(n *node, a ast.Node) {
	*s = append(*s, astNode{n, a})
}

func (s *nodestack) pop() astNode {
	l := len(*s) - 1
	res := (*s)[l]
	*s = (*s)[:l]
	return res
}

func (s *nodestack) top() astNode {
	l := len(*s)
	if l > 0 {
		return (*s)[l-1]
	}
	return astNode{}
}

// dup returns a duplicated node subtree.
func (interp *Interpreter) dup(nod, anc *node) *node {
	nindex := atomic.AddInt64(&interp.nindex, 1)

			case anc.node.kind == varDecl && anc.node.anc.kind != fileStmt:
				kind, act = defineStmt, aAssign
			}
			n := addChild(&root, anc, pos, kind, act)
			n.nleft = len(a.Names)
			n.nright = len(a.Values)
			st.push(n, nod)


END STEP

cd /app && cat > interp/embed.go <<'EOF'
package interp

import (
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"go/ast"
	"io"
	"io/fs"
	"path"
	"reflect"
	"sort"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"time"
)

// embedData stores the resolved files of a //go:embed directive.
type embedData struct {
	dir      string
	patterns []string
	files    map[string][]byte
}

// embedPatterns extracts the //go:embed directives from a comment group.
// It returns the list of patterns found in all the embed lines.
func embedPatterns(cg *ast.CommentGroup) ([]string, error) {
	if cg == nil {
		return nil, nil
	}
	var patterns []string
	for _, c := range cg.List {
		text := c.Text
		if len(text) < len("//go:embed") || text[:len("//go:embed")] != "//go:embed" {
			continue
		}
		rest := text[len("//go:embed"):]
		if rest != "" && rest[0] != ' ' && rest[0] != '\t' {
			// Not a go:embed directive, just a comment starting with go:embed.
			continue
		}
		ps, err := parseEmbedPatterns(rest)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		patterns = append(patterns, ps...)
	}
	return patterns, nil
}

// parseEmbedPatterns splits a go:embed line into its patterns. Quoted
// patterns are supported as in the Go specification.
func parseEmbedPatterns(s string) ([]string, error) {
	s = strings.TrimSpace(s)
	if s == "" {
		return nil, errors.New("go:embed: empty pattern")
	}
	var patterns []string
	for len(s) > 0 {
		switch s[0] {
		case '"', '`':
			// Find the end of the quoted string.
			end := 1
			for end < len(s) {
				if s[0] == '"' && s[end] == '\\' {
					end += 2
					continue
				}
				if s[end] == s[0] {
					break
				}
				end++
			}
			if end >= len(s) {
				return nil, errors.New("go:embed: unterminated quoted pattern")
			}
			p, err := strconv.Unquote(s[:end+1])
			if err != nil {
				return nil, err
			}
			patterns = append(patterns, p)
			s = strings.TrimSpace(s[end+1:])
		default:
			end := strings.IndexAny(s, " \t\r\n")
			if end < 0 {
				end = len(s)
			}
			patterns = append(patterns, s[:end])
			s = strings.TrimSpace(s[end:])
		}
	}
	return patterns, nil
}

// splitAll returns the pattern without the "all:" prefix and a boolean
// indicating whether the prefix was present.
func splitAll(pattern string) (string, bool) {
	if strings.HasPrefix(pattern, "all:") {
		return pattern[4:], true
	}
	return pattern, false
}

// validateEmbedPattern checks that a pattern is valid for use in a
// //go:embed directive.
func validateEmbedPattern(pattern string) error {
	if pattern == "" {
		return errors.New("go:embed: empty pattern")
	}
	if pattern == "." || pattern == ".." {
		return fmt.Errorf("go:embed: invalid pattern %q", pattern)
	}
	if strings.HasPrefix(pattern, "/") || strings.HasSuffix(pattern, "/") {
		return fmt.Errorf("go:embed: invalid pattern %q", pattern)
	}
	if strings.Contains(pattern, "\\") {
		return fmt.Errorf("go:embed: invalid pattern %q", pattern)
	}
	for _, part := range strings.Split(pattern, "/") {
		if part == "" || part == "." || part == ".." {
			return fmt.Errorf("go:embed: invalid pattern %q", pattern)
		}
	}
	if _, err := path.Match(pattern, "dummy"); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("go:embed: invalid pattern %q: %v", pattern, err)
	}
	return nil
}

// visible reports whether a relative path contains no hidden component
// (a component beginning with '.' or '_').
func visible(name string) bool {
	for _, part := range strings.Split(name, "/") {
		if strings.HasPrefix(part, ".") || strings.HasPrefix(part, "_") {
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}

// collectEmbedEntries walks the directory dir and returns the relative paths
// of all directories and regular files it contains.
func collectEmbedEntries(fsys fs.FS, dir string) (dirs, files map[string]bool, err error) {
	dirs = map[string]bool{}
	files = map[string]bool{}
	var walk func(root, rel string) error
	walk = func(root, rel string) error {
		entries, err := fs.ReadDir(fsys, root)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		for _, e := range entries {
			childRel := e.Name()
			if rel != "" {
				childRel = path.Join(rel, e.Name())
			}
			childRoot := path.Join(root, e.Name())
			if e.IsDir() {
				dirs[childRel] = true
				if err := walk(childRoot, childRel); err != nil {
					return err
				}
			} else {
				files[childRel] = true
			}
		}
		return nil
	}
	if err := walk(dir, ""); err != nil {
		return nil, nil, err
	}
	return dirs, files, nil
}

// resolveEmbed resolves the patterns against the source directory dir and
// reads the matching files from the interpreter source filesystem.
func (interp *Interpreter) resolveEmbed(dir string, patterns []string) (*embedData, error) {
	dirs, files, err := collectEmbedEntries(interp.opt.filesystem, dir)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	data := &embedData{dir: dir, patterns: patterns, files: map[string][]byte{}}

	for _, raw := range patterns {
		all, pattern := splitAll(raw)
		if err := validateEmbedPattern(pattern); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		matched := false
		add := func(rel string) error {
			if _, ok := data.files[rel]; ok {
				return nil
			}
			full := path.Join(dir, rel)
			b, err := fs.ReadFile(interp.opt.filesystem, full)
			if err != nil {
				return err
			}
			data.files[rel] = b
			matched = true
			return nil
		}

		// Match directories: embed the whole subtree.
		for d := range dirs {
			ok, err := path.Match(pattern, d)
			if err != nil {
				return nil, err
			}
			if !ok {
				continue
			}
			prefix := d + "/"
			for f := range files {
				if f == d || strings.HasPrefix(f, prefix) {
					if all || visible(f) {
						if err := add(f); err != nil {
							return nil, err
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		// Match files.
		for f := range files {
			ok, err := path.Match(pattern, f)
			if err != nil {
				return nil, err
			}
			if !ok {
				continue
			}
			if all || visible(f) {
				if err := add(f); err != nil {
					return nil, err
				}
			}
		}

		if !matched {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("go:embed: pattern %q matched no files", raw)
		}
	}

	return data, nil
}

// genEmbedVar generates the initialization of a global variable declared with
// a //go:embed directive.
func genEmbedVar(n *node) {
	next := getExec(n.tnext)
	data := n.embed
	if data == nil {
		n.gen = nop
		n.gen(n)
		return
	}

	// The embedded declaration must declare a single variable.
	if len(n.child) < 2 || n.nleft != 1 {
		n.exec = func(f *frame) bltn {
			panic(n.cfgErrorf("go:embed cannot apply to var with multiple names"))
		}
		return
	}
	dest := n.child[0]
	index := dest.findex
	level := dest.level
	rt := n.typ.frameType()

	n.exec = func(f *frame) bltn {
		v, err := embedValue(data, rt)
		if err != nil {
			panic(n.cfgErrorf("go:embed: %v", err))
		}
		getFrame(f, level).data[index].Set(v)
		return next
	}
}

// embedValue builds the reflect.Value of the embedded content for the given
// target type.
func embedValue(data *embedData, rt reflect.Type) (reflect.Value, error) {
	switch rt.Kind() {
	case reflect.String:
		if len(data.files) != 1 {
			return reflect.Value{}, fmt.Errorf("pattern matches %d files, but must match exactly one file for a string", len(data.files))
		}
		for _, b := range data.files {
			return reflect.ValueOf(string(b)).Convert(rt), nil
		}
	case reflect.Slice:
		if rt.Elem().Kind() != reflect.Uint8 {
			return reflect.Value{}, fmt.Errorf("go:embed cannot apply to var of type %s", rt)
		}
		if len(data.files) != 1 {
			return reflect.Value{}, fmt.Errorf("pattern matches %d files, but must match exactly one file for a []byte", len(data.files))
		}
		for _, b := range data.files {
			return reflect.ValueOf(b).Convert(rt), nil
		}
	}

	if rt == reflect.TypeOf(EmbedFS{}) {
		fs := EmbedFS{files: data.files}
		return reflect.ValueOf(fs), nil
	}

	return reflect.Value{}, fmt.Errorf("go:embed cannot apply to var of type %s", rt)
}

// EmbedFS is a read-only in-memory filesystem built from //go:embed
// directives. It implements fs.FS, fs.ReadFileFS and fs.ReadDirFS.
type EmbedFS struct {
	files map[string][]byte
}

var (
	_ fs.FS         = (*EmbedFS)(nil)
	_ fs.ReadFileFS = (*EmbedFS)(nil)
	_ fs.ReadDirFS  = (*EmbedFS)(nil)
)

// Open opens the named file or directory for reading.
func (f *EmbedFS) Open(name string) (fs.File, error) {
	if name == "" {
		name = "."
	}
	if !fs.ValidPath(name) && name != "." {
		return nil, &fs.PathError{Op: "open", Path: name, Err: fs.ErrInvalid}
	}
	name = path.Clean(name)
	isDir, ok := f.lookup(name)
	if !ok {
		return nil, &fs.PathError{Op: "open", Path: name, Err: fs.ErrNotExist}
	}
	if isDir {
		return &embedDir{name: name, entries: f.readDir(name)}, nil
	}
	return &embedFile{name: name, data: append([]byte(nil), f.files[name]...)}, nil
}

// ReadFile reads the named file and returns its contents. A fresh copy is
// returned on every call.
func (f *EmbedFS) ReadFile(name string) ([]byte, error) {
	if !fs.ValidPath(name) {
		return nil, &fs.PathError{Op: "read", Path: name, Err: fs.ErrInvalid}
	}
	name = path.Clean(name)
	isDir, ok := f.lookup(name)
	if !ok {
		return nil, &fs.PathError{Op: "read", Path: name, Err: fs.ErrNotExist}
	}
	if isDir {
		return nil, &fs.PathError{Op: "read", Path: name, Err: errors.New("is a directory")}
	}
	return append([]byte(nil), f.files[name]...), nil
}

// ReadDir reads the named directory and returns a list of directory entries
// sorted by filename.
func (f *EmbedFS) ReadDir(name string) ([]fs.DirEntry, error) {
	if name == "" {
		name = "."
	}
	if !fs.ValidPath(name) && name != "." {
		return nil, &fs.PathError{Op: "read", Path: name, Err: fs.ErrInvalid}
	}
	name = path.Clean(name)
	isDir, ok := f.lookup(name)
	if !ok {
		return nil, &fs.PathError{Op: "read", Path: name, Err: fs.ErrNotExist}
	}
	if !isDir {
		return nil, &fs.PathError{Op: "read", Path: name, Err: errors.New("not a directory")}
	}
	return f.readDir(name), nil
}

func (f *EmbedFS) lookup(name string) (isDir, ok bool) {
	if f.files == nil {
		return false, false
	}
	name = strings.TrimSuffix(name, "/")
	if name == "." {
		return true, len(f.files) > 0
	}
	if _, ok := f.files[name]; ok {
		return false, true
	}
	prefix := name + "/"
	for p := range f.files {
		if strings.HasPrefix(p, prefix) {
			return true, true
		}
	}
	return false, false
}

func (f *EmbedFS) readDir(name string) []fs.DirEntry {
	prefix := ""
	if name != "." {
		prefix = name + "/"
	}
	type entry struct {
		base  string
		isDir bool
	}
	m := map[string]entry{}
	for p := range f.files {
		if !strings.HasPrefix(p, prefix) {
			continue
		}
		rest := p[len(prefix):]
		if rest == "" {
			continue
		}
		base, sub, _ := strings.Cut(rest, "/")
		e, ok := m[base]
		if !ok {
			e = entry{base: base}
		}
		if sub != "" {
			e.isDir = true
		}
		m[base] = e
	}
	names := make([]string, 0, len(m))
	for base := range m {
		names = append(names, base)
	}
	sort.Strings(names)
	entries := make([]fs.DirEntry, len(names))
	for i, base := range names {
		e := m[base]
		entries[i] = &embedDirEntry{name: e.base, isDir: e.isDir, size: 0}
	}
	return entries
}

type embedFile struct {
	name   string
	data   []byte
	offset int64
}

func (f *embedFile) Close() error { return nil }

func (f *embedFile) Read(p []byte) (int, error) {
	if f.offset >= int64(len(f.data)) {
		return 0, io.EOF
	}
	n := copy(p, f.data[f.offset:])
	f.offset += int64(n)
	return n, nil
}

func (f *embedFile) Stat() (fs.FileInfo, error) {
	return &embedFileInfo{name: f.name, size: int64(len(f.data))}, nil
}

func (f *embedFile) Seek(offset int64, whence int) (int64, error) {
	switch whence {
	case io.SeekStart:
		// offset unchanged
	case io.SeekCurrent:
		offset += f.offset
	case io.SeekEnd:
		offset += int64(len(f.data))
	default:
		return 0, &fs.PathError{Op: "seek", Path: f.name, Err: fs.ErrInvalid}
	}
	if offset < 0 || offset > int64(len(f.data)) {
		return 0, &fs.PathError{Op: "seek", Path: f.name, Err: fs.ErrInvalid}
	}
	f.offset = offset
	return offset, nil
}

type embedDir struct {
	name    string
	entries []fs.DirEntry
	offset  int
}

func (d *embedDir) Close() error { return nil }

func (d *embedDir) Read([]byte) (int, error) {
	return 0, &fs.PathError{Op: "read", Path: d.name, Err: errors.New("is a directory")}
}

func (d *embedDir) Stat() (fs.FileInfo, error) {
	return &embedFileInfo{name: d.name, isDir: true}, nil
}

func (d *embedDir) ReadDir(n int) ([]fs.DirEntry, error) {
	if n <= 0 {
		d.offset = 0
		res := make([]fs.DirEntry, len(d.entries))
		copy(res, d.entries)
		return res, nil
	}
	if d.offset >= len(d.entries) {
		return nil, io.EOF
	}
	res := d.entries[d.offset:]
	if n < len(res) {
		res = res[:n]
	}
	d.offset += len(res)
	return res, nil
}

type embedDirEntry struct {
	name  string
	isDir bool
	size  int64
}

func (e *embedDirEntry) Name() string               { return e.name }
func (e *embedDirEntry) IsDir() bool                { return e.isDir }
func (e *embedDirEntry) Type() fs.FileMode          { if e.isDir { return fs.ModeDir }; return 0 }
func (e *embedDirEntry) Info() (fs.FileInfo, error) { return &embedFileInfo{name: e.name, isDir: e.isDir, size: e.size}, nil }

type embedFileInfo struct {
	name  string
	isDir bool
	size  int64
}

func (i *embedFileInfo) Name() string       { return i.name }
func (i *embedFileInfo) Size() int64        { return i.size }
func (i *embedFileInfo) Mode() fs.FileMode  { if i.isDir { return fs.ModeDir }; return 0 }
func (i *embedFileInfo) ModTime() time.Time { return time.Time{} }
func (i *embedFileInfo) IsDir() bool        { return i.isDir }
func (i *embedFileInfo) Sys() interface{}   { return nil }
EOF

// embedDirFor returns the directory of the source file associated with the
// given position.
func (interp *Interpreter) embedDirFor(pos token.Pos) string {
	filename := interp.fset.Position(pos).Filename
	if filename == "" {
		return "."
	}
	return path.Dir(filename)
}

func genGlobalVarDecl(nodes []*node, sc *scope) (*node, error) {
	varNode := &node{kind: varDecl, action: aNop, gen: nop}

	deps := map[*node][]*node{}
	for _, n := range nodes {
		deps[n] = getVarDependencies(n, sc)
	}

	inited := map[*node]bool{}
	revisit := []*node{}
	for {
		for _, n := range nodes {
			canInit := true
			for _, d := range deps[n] {
				if !inited[d] {
					canInit = false
				}
			}
			if !canInit {
				revisit = append(revisit, n)
				continue
			}

			varNode.child = append(varNode.child, n)
			inited[n] = true
		}

		if len(revisit) == 0 || equalNodes(nodes, revisit) {
			break
		}

		nodes = revisit
		revisit = []*node{}
	}

	if len(revisit) > 0 {
		return nil, revisit[0].cfgErrorf("variable definition loop")
	}
	wireChild(varNode)
	return varNode, nil
}

func getVarDependencies(nod *node, sc *scope) (deps []*node) {
	nod.Walk(func(n *node) bool {
		if n.kind != identExpr {
			return true
		}
		// Process ident nodes, and avoid false dependencies.
		if n.anc.kind == selectorExpr && childPos(n) == 1 {
			return false
		}
		sym, _, ok := sc.lookup(n.ident)
		if !ok {

END STEP

// opt stores interpreter options.
type opt struct {
	// dotCmd is the command to process the dot graph produced when astDot and/or
	// cfgDot is enabled. It defaults to 'dot -Tdot -o <filename>.dot'.
	dotCmd       string
	context      build.Context     // build context: GOPATH, build constraints
	stdin        io.Reader         // standard input
	stdout       io.Writer         // standard output
	stderr       io.Writer         // standard error
	args         []string          // cmdline args
	env          map[string]string // environment of interpreter, entries in form of "key=value"
	filesystem   fs.FS             // filesystem containing sources
	astDot       bool              // display AST graph (debug)
	cfgDot       bool              // display CFG graph (debug)
	noRun        bool              // compile, but do not run
	fastChan     bool              // disable cancellable chan operations
	specialStdio bool              // allows os.Stdin, os.Stdout, os.Stderr to not be file descriptors
	unrestricted bool              // allow use of non-sandboxed symbols
}

	f := &frame{
		anc:  anc,
		data: make([]reflect.Value, length),
		id:   id,

func (f *frame) runid() uint64      { return atomic.LoadUint64(&f.id) }
func (f *frame) setrunid(id uint64) { atomic.StoreUint64(&f.id, id) }
func (f *frame) clone() *frame {
	f.mutex.RLock()
	defer f.mutex.RUnlock()
	nf := &frame{
		anc:       f.anc,
		root:      f.root,
		deferred:  f.deferred,
		recovered: f.recovered,
		id:        f.runid(),
		done:      f.done,
		debug:     f.debug,
	}
	nf.data = make([]reflect.Value, len(f.data))
	copy(nf.data, f.data)
	return nf
}

type imports map[string]map[string]*symbol

// Package embedfs provides a read-only in-memory filesystem used to back
// //go:embed directives in the interpreter.
package embedfs

import (
	"errors"
	"io"
	"io/fs"
	"path"
	"sort"
	"strings"
	"time"
)

// FS is a read-only in-memory filesystem built from //go:embed directives.
// It implements fs.FS, fs.ReadFileFS and fs.ReadDirFS.
type FS struct {
	files map[string][]byte
}

var (
	_ fs.FS         = (*FS)(nil)
	_ fs.ReadFileFS = (*FS)(nil)
	_ fs.ReadDirFS  = (*FS)(nil)
)

// New returns a read-only filesystem containing the given files.
// The keys of files are slash-separated paths relative to the filesystem root.
func New(files map[string][]byte) FS {
	return FS{files: files}
}

// Open opens the named file or directory for reading.
func (f *FS) Open(name string) (fs.File, error) {
	if name == "" {
		name = "."
	}
	if !fs.ValidPath(name) && name != "." {
		return nil, &fs.PathError{Op: "open", Path: name, Err: fs.ErrInvalid}
	}
	name = path.Clean(name)
	isDir, ok := f.lookup(name)
	if !ok {
		return nil, &fs.PathError{Op: "open", Path: name, Err: fs.ErrNotExist}
	}
	if isDir {
		return &embedDir{name: name, entries: f.readDir(name)}, nil
	}
	return &embedFile{name: name, data: append([]byte(nil), f.files[name]...)}, nil
}

// ReadFile reads the named file and returns its contents. A fresh copy is
// returned on every call.
func (f *FS) ReadFile(name string) ([]byte, error) {
	if !fs.ValidPath(name) {
		return nil, &fs.PathError{Op: "read", Path: name, Err: fs.ErrInvalid}
	}
	name = path.Clean(name)
	isDir, ok := f.lookup(name)
	if !ok {
		return nil, &fs.PathError{Op: "read", Path: name, Err: fs.ErrNotExist}
	}
	if isDir {
		return nil, &fs.PathError{Op: "read", Path: name, Err: errors.New("is a directory")}
	}
	return append([]byte(nil), f.files[name]...), nil
}

// ReadDir reads the named directory and returns a list of directory entries
// sorted by filename.
func (f *FS) ReadDir(name string) ([]fs.DirEntry, error) {
	if name == "" {
		name = "."
	}
	if !fs.ValidPath(name) && name != "." {
		return nil, &fs.PathError{Op: "read", Path: name, Err: fs.ErrInvalid}
	}
	name = path.Clean(name)
	isDir, ok := f.lookup(name)
	if !ok {
		return nil, &fs.PathError{Op: "read", Path: name, Err: fs.ErrNotExist}
	}
	if !isDir {
		return nil, &fs.PathError{Op: "read", Path: name, Err: errors.New("not a directory")}
	}
	return f.readDir(name), nil
}

func (f *FS) lookup(name string) (isDir, ok bool) {
	if f.files == nil {
		return false, false
	}
	name = strings.TrimSuffix(name, "/")
	if name == "." {
		return true, len(f.files) > 0
	}
	if _, ok := f.files[name]; ok {
		return false, true
	}
	prefix := name + "/"
	for p := range f.files {
		if strings.HasPrefix(p, prefix) {
			return true, true
		}
	}
	return false, false
}

func (f *FS) readDir(name string) []fs.DirEntry {
	prefix := ""
	if name != "." {
		prefix = name + "/"
	}
	type entry struct {
		base  string
		isDir bool
	}
	m := map[string]entry{}
	for p := range f.files {
		if !strings.HasPrefix(p, prefix) {
			continue
		}
		rest := p[len(prefix):]
		if rest == "" {
			continue
		}
		base, sub, _ := strings.Cut(rest, "/")
		e, ok := m[base]
		if !ok {
			e = entry{base: base}
		}
		if sub != "" {
			e.isDir = true
		}
		m[base] = e
	}
	names := make([]string, 0, len(m))
	for base := range m {
		names = append(names, base)
	}
	sort.Strings(names)
	entries := make([]fs.DirEntry, len(names))
	for i, base := range names {
		e := m[base]
		entries[i] = &embedDirEntry{name: e.base, isDir: e.isDir, size: 0}
	}
	return entries
}

type embedFile struct {
	name   string
	data   []byte
	offset int64
}

func (f *embedFile) Close() error { return nil }

func (f *embedFile) Read(p []byte) (int, error) {
	if f.offset >= int64(len(f.data)) {
		return 0, io.EOF
	}
	n := copy(p, f.data[f.offset:])
	f.offset += int64(n)
	return n, nil
}

func (f *embedFile) Stat() (fs.FileInfo, error) {
	return &embedFileInfo{name: f.name, size: int64(len(f.data))}, nil
}

func (f *embedFile) Seek(offset int64, whence int) (int64, error) {
	switch whence {
	case io.SeekStart:
		// offset unchanged
	case io.SeekCurrent:
		offset += f.offset
	case io.SeekEnd:
		offset += int64(len(f.data))
	default:
		return 0, &fs.PathError{Op: "seek", Path: f.name, Err: fs.ErrInvalid}
	}
	if offset < 0 || offset > int64(len(f.data)) {
		return 0, &fs.PathError{Op: "seek", Path: f.name, Err: fs.ErrInvalid}
	}
	f.offset = offset
	return offset, nil
}

type embedDir struct {
	name    string
	entries []fs.DirEntry
	offset  int
}

func (d *embedDir) Close() error { return nil }

func (d *embedDir) Read([]byte) (int, error) {
	return 0, &fs.PathError{Op: "read", Path: d.name, Err: errors.New("is a directory")}
}

func (d *embedDir) Stat() (fs.FileInfo, error) {
	return &embedFileInfo{name: d.name, isDir: true}, nil
}

func (d *embedDir) ReadDir(n int) ([]fs.DirEntry, error) {
	if n <= 0 {
		d.offset = 0
		res := make([]fs.DirEntry, len(d.entries))
		copy(res, d.entries)
		return res, nil
	}
	if d.offset >= len(d.entries) {
		return nil, io.EOF
	}
	res := d.entries[d.offset:]
	if n < len(res) {
		res = res[:n]
	}
	d.offset += len(res)
	return res, nil
}

type embedDirEntry struct {
	name  string
	isDir bool
	size  int64
}

func (e *embedDirEntry) Name() string               { return e.name }
func (e *embedDirEntry) IsDir() bool                { return e.isDir }
func (e *embedDirEntry) Type() fs.FileMode          { if e.isDir { return fs.ModeDir }; return 0 }
func (e *embedDirEntry) Info() (fs.FileInfo, error) { return &embedFileInfo{name: e.name, isDir: e.isDir, size: e.size}, nil }

type embedFileInfo struct {
	name  string
	isDir bool
	size  int64
}

func (i *embedFileInfo) Name() string       { return i.name }
func (i *embedFileInfo) Size() int64        { return i.size }
func (i *embedFileInfo) Mode() fs.FileMode  { if i.isDir { return fs.ModeDir }; return 0 }
func (i *embedFileInfo) ModTime() time.Time { return time.Time{} }
func (i *embedFileInfo) IsDir() bool        { return i.isDir }
func (i *embedFileInfo) Sys() interface{}   { return nil }
EOF

package interp

import (
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"go/ast"
	"go/token"
	"io"
	"io/fs"
	"path"

	"github.com/traefik/yaegi/internal/embedfs"
	"reflect"
	"sort"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"time"
)

// embedData stores the resolved files of a //go:embed directive.
type embedData struct {
	dir      string
	patterns []string
	files    map[string][]byte
}

// embedPatterns extracts the //go:embed directives from a comment group.
// It returns the list of patterns found in all the embed lines.
func embedPatterns(cg *ast.CommentGroup) ([]string, error) {
	if cg == nil {
		return nil, nil
	}
	var patterns []string
	for _, c := range cg.List {
		text := c.Text
		if len(text) < len("//go:embed") || text[:len("//go:embed")] != "//go:embed" {
			continue
		}
		rest := text[len("//go:embed"):]
		if rest != "" && rest[0] != ' ' && rest[0] != '\t' {
			// Not a go:embed directive, just a comment starting with go:embed.
			continue
		}
		ps, err := parseEmbedPatterns(rest)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		patterns = append(patterns, ps...)
	}
	return patterns, nil

STEP 82 TOOL 1
COMMAND:
cd /app && cat > interp/zz_repro_embed_test.go <<'EOF'
package interp_test

import (
	"bytes"
	"io/fs"
	"testing"
	"testing/fstest"

	"github.com/traefik/yaegi/interp"
	"github.com/traefik/yaegi/stdlib"
)

func runEmbed(t *testing.T, fsys fs.FS, src string) (string, error) {
	t.Helper()
	i := interp.New(interp.Options{SourcecodeFilesystem: fsys})
	if err := i.Use(stdlib.Symbols); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var stdout bytes.Buffer
	i.Stdout = &stdout
	_, err := i.EvalPath("main.go")
	return stdout.String(), err
}

func TestEmbedString(t *testing.T) {
	fsys := fstest.MapFS{
		"main.go": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte(`package main
import (
	_ "embed"
	"fmt"
)
//go:embed hello.txt
var s string
func main() { fmt.Println(s) }
`)},
		"hello.txt": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte("hello world")},
	}
	out, err := runEmbed(t, fsys, "")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if out != "hello world\n" {
		t.Fatalf("got %q", out)
	}
}

func TestEmbedBytes(t *testing.T) {
	fsys := fstest.MapFS{
		"main.go": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte(`package main
import (
	_ "embed"
	"fmt"
)
//go:embed hello.txt
var b []byte
func main() { fmt.Println(string(b)) }
`)},
		"hello.txt": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte("hello bytes")},
	}
	out, err := runEmbed(t, fsys, "")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if out != "hello bytes\n" {
		t.Fatalf("got %q", out)
	}
}

func TestEmbedFS(t *testing.T) {
	fsys := fstest.MapFS{
		"main.go": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte(`package main
import (
	"embed"
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
)
//go:embed data
var content embed.FS
func main() {
	b, err := content.ReadFile("data/a.txt")
	if err != nil { fmt.Println("ERR", err); return }
	fmt.Println(string(b))
	entries, _ := fs.ReadDir(content, "data")
	fmt.Println(len(entries), entries[0].Name())
	var _ fs.FS = content
	var _ fs.ReadFileFS = content
	var _ fs.ReadDirFS = content
}
`)},
		"data/a.txt":   &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte("A")},
		"data/b.txt":   &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte("B")},
		"data/sub/c.txt": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte("C")},
	}
	out, err := runEmbed(t, fsys, "")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if out != "A\n2 a.txt\n" {
		t.Fatalf("got %q", out)
	}
}

func TestEmbedGrouped(t *testing.T) {
	fsys := fstest.MapFS{
		"main.go": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte(`package main
import (
	_ "embed"
	"fmt"
)
//go:embed a.txt
var (
	x string
	//go:embed b.txt
	y string
)
func main() { fmt.Println(x, y) }
`)},
		"a.txt": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte("A")},
		"b.txt": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte("B")},
	}
	out, err := runEmbed(t, fsys, "")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if out != "A B\n" {
		t.Fatalf("got %q", out)
	}
}

func TestEmbedDirAndHidden(t *testing.T) {
	fsys := fstest.MapFS{
		"main.go": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte(`package main
import (
	"embed"
	"fmt"
)
//go:embed all:data
var content embed.FS
func main() {
	entries, _ := content.ReadDir("data")
	fmt.Println(len(entries), entries[0].Name())
	b, err := content.ReadFile("data/.hidden")
	if err == nil { fmt.Println("hidden", string(b)) } else { fmt.Println("hidden ERR") }
}
`)},
		"data/a.txt":      &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte("A")},
		"data/.hidden":    &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte("H")},
	}
	out, err := runEmbed(t, fsys, "")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if out != "2 .hidden\nhidden H\n" {
		t.Fatalf("got %q", out)
	}
}

func TestEmbedErrors(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		name    string
		files   map[string]string
		wantErr string
	}{
		{
			name: "no files",
			files: map[string]string{
				"main.go": `package main
import _ "embed"
//go:embed missing.txt
var s string
func main() {}
`,
			},
			wantErr: "no files",
		},
		{
			name: "multiple files for string",
			files: map[string]string{
				"main.go": `package main
import _ "embed"
//go:embed data
var s string
func main() {}
`,
				"data/a.txt": "A",
				"data/b.txt": "B",
			},
			wantErr: "exactly one file",
		},
		{
			name: "initializer",
			files: map[string]string{
				"main.go": `package main
import _ "embed"
//go:embed a.txt
var s = "x"
func main() {}
`,
				"a.txt": "A",
			},
			wantErr: "initializer",
		},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			fsys := fstest.MapFS{}
			for k, v := range tc.files {
				fsys[k] = &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte(v)}
			}
			_, err := runEmbed(t, fsys, "")
			if err == nil || !bytes.Contains([]byte(err.Error()), []byte(tc.wantErr)) {
				t.Fatalf("got err %v, want containing %q", err, tc.wantErr)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestEmbedNotOverwritten(t *testing.T) {
	fsys := fstest.MapFS{
		"main.go": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte(`package main
import (
	_ "embed"
	"fmt"
)
//go:embed a.txt
var s string
var copy = s
func main() { fmt.Println(s, copy) }
`)},
		"a.txt": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte("AA")},
	}
	out, err := runEmbed(t, fsys, "")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if out != "AA AA\n" {
		t.Fatalf("got %q", out)
	}
}

returncode=0
		case *ast.ValueSpec:
			kind := valueSpec
			act := aNop
			switch {
			case a.Values != nil:
				if len(a.Names) > 1 && len(a.Values) == 1 {
					if anc.node.kind == constDecl || anc.node.kind == varDecl {
						kind = defineXStmt
					} else {
						kind = assignXStmt
					}
					act = aAssignX
				} else {
					if anc.node.kind == constDecl || anc.node.kind == varDecl {
						kind = defineStmt
					} else {
						kind = assignStmt
					}
					act = aAssign
				}
			case anc.node.kind == constDecl:
				kind, act = defineStmt, aAssign
			case anc.node.kind == varDecl && anc.node.anc.kind != fileStmt:
				kind, act = defineStmt, aAssign
			}
			n := addChild(&root, anc, pos, kind, act)
			n.nleft = len(a.Names)
			n.nright = len(a.Values)
			if anc.node != nil && anc.node.kind == varDecl {
				patterns, err := embedPatterns(a.Doc)
				if err != nil {
					return false
				}
				if len(patterns) == 0 && anc.node.embed != nil && len(anc.node.embed.patterns) > 0 {
					patterns = anc.node.embed.patterns
					anc.node.embed.patterns = nil
				}
				if len(patterns) > 0 {
					if anc.node.anc.kind != fileStmt {
						err = astError(fmt.Errorf("go:embed cannot apply to a local variable"))
						return false
					}
					if len(a.Names) > 1 {
						err = astError(fmt.Errorf("go:embed cannot apply to var with multiple names"))
						return false
					}
					if a.Values != nil {
						err = astError(fmt.Errorf("go:embed cannot apply to var with initializer"))
						return false
					}
					dir := interp.embedDirFor(pos)
					data, err := interp.resolveEmbed(dir, patterns)
					if err != nil {
						err = astError(err)
						return false
					}
					n.embed = data
				}
			}
			st.push(n, nod)

		default:
			err = astError(fmt.Errorf("ast: %T not implemented, line %s", a, interp.fset.Position(pos)))
			return false
		}
		return true
	})

	interp.roots = append(interp.roots, root)
	return pkgName, root, err

returncode=0
func (t *itype) assignableTo(o *itype) bool {
	if t.equals(o) {
		return true
	}

	if t.cat == linkedT && o.cat == linkedT && (t.underlying().id() != o.underlying().id() || !typeDefined(t, o)) {
		return false
	}

	if t.isNil() && o.hasNil() || o.isNil() && t.hasNil() {
		return true
	}

	if t.TypeOf().AssignableTo(o.TypeOf()) {
		return true
	}

	if isInterface(o) && t.implements(o) {
		return true
	}

	if t.cat == sliceT && o.cat == sliceT {
		return t.val.assignableTo(o.val)
	}

	if t.isBinMethod && isFunc(o) {
		// TODO (marc): check that t without receiver as first parameter is equivalent to o.
		return true
	}

	if t.untyped && isNumber(t.TypeOf()) && isNumber(o.TypeOf()) {
		// Assignability depends on constant numeric value (overflow check), to be tested elsewhere.
		return true
	}

	n := t.node
	if n == nil || !n.rval.IsValid() {
		return false
	}
	con, ok := n.rval.Interface().(constant.Value)
	if !ok {
		return false
	}
	if con == nil || !isConstType(o) {
		return false
	}
	return representableConst(con, o.TypeOf())
}

// convertibleTo returns true if t is convertible to o.
func (t *itype) convertibleTo(o *itype) bool {
	if t.assignableTo(o) {
		return true
	}

	// unsafe checks
	tt, ot := t.TypeOf(), o.TypeOf()
	if (tt.Kind() == reflect.Ptr || tt.Kind() == reflect.Uintptr) && ot.Kind() == reflect.UnsafePointer {
		return true
	}
	if tt.Kind() == reflect.UnsafePointer && (ot.Kind() == reflect.Ptr || ot.Kind() == reflect.Uintptr) {
		return true
	}

	return t.TypeOf().ConvertibleTo(o.TypeOf())
}

// ordered returns true if the type is ordered.
func (t *itype) ordered() bool {
=== implements ===
func (t *itype) implements(it *itype) bool {
	if isBin(t) {
		// Note: in case of a valueInterfaceType, we
		// miss required data which will be available
		// later, so we optimistically return true to progress,
		// and additional checks will be hopefully performed at
		// runtime.
		if rt := it.TypeOf(); rt == valueInterfaceType {
			return true
		}
		return t.TypeOf().Implements(it.TypeOf())
	}
	return t.methods().contains(it.methods())
}

// defaultType returns the default type of an untyped type.
func (t *itype) defaultType(v reflect.Value, sc *scope) *itype {
	if !t.untyped {
		return t
	}

	typ := t
	// The default type can also be derived from a constant value.
	if v.IsValid() && v.Type().Implements(constVal) {
		switch v.Interface().(constant.Value).Kind() {
		case constant.String:
			typ = sc.getType("string")
		case constant.Bool:
			typ = sc.getType("bool")
		case constant.Int:
			switch t.cat {
			case int32T:
				typ = sc.getType("int32")
			default:
				typ = sc.getType("int")
			}
		case constant.Float:
			typ = sc.getType("float64")
		case constant.Complex:
			typ = sc.getType("complex128")
		}
	}

END STEP

205:func valueTOf(rtype reflect.Type, opts ...itypeOption) *itype {
func valueTOf(rtype reflect.Type, opts ...itypeOption) *itype {
	t := &itype{cat: valueT, rtype: rtype, str: rtype.String()}
	for _, opt := range opts {
		opt(t)
	}
	if t.untyped {
		t.str = "untyped " + t.str
	}
	return t
}

// wrapperValueTOf returns a valueT itype wrapping an itype.
func wrapperValueTOf(rtype reflect.Type, val *itype, opts ...itypeOption) *itype {
	t := &itype{cat: valueT, rtype: rtype, val: val, str: rtype.String()}
	for _, opt := range opts {
		opt(t)
	}
	return t
}

func variadicOf(val *itype, opts ...itypeOption) *itype {
	t := &itype{cat: variadicT, val: val, str: "..." + val.str}
	for _, opt := range opts {
		opt(t)
	}
	return t
}

// ptrOf returns a pointer to t.
func ptrOf(val *itype, opts ...itypeOption) *itype {
	if val.ptr != nil {
		return val.ptr
	}
	t := &itype{cat: ptrT, val: val, str: "*" + val.str}
	for _, opt := range opts {
		opt(t)
	}
	val.ptr = t
	return t
}

returncode=0
func isBin(t *itype) bool {
	switch t.cat {
	case valueT:
		return true
	case linkedT, ptrT:
		return isBin(t.val)
	default:
		return false
	}
}

STEP 107 TOOL 1
COMMAND:
cd /app && sed -n '2277,2310p' interp/cfg.go
OBSERVATION:
returncode=0
		case valueSpec:
			n.gen = reset
			l := len(n.child) - 1
			if n.typ = n.child[l].typ; n.typ == nil {
				if n.typ, err = nodeType(interp, sc, n.child[l]); err != nil {
					return
				}
			}

			for _, c := range n.child[:l] {
				var index int
				if sc.global {
					// Global object allocation is already performed in GTA.
					index = sc.sym[c.ident].index
					c.level = globalFrame
				} else {
					index = sc.add(n.typ)
					sc.sym[c.ident] = &symbol{index: index, kind: varSym, typ: n.typ}
				}
				c.typ = n.typ
				c.findex = index
			}
		}
	})

	if sc != interp.universe {
		sc.pop()
	}
	return initNodes, err
}

func TestEmbedMultipleLines(t *testing.T) {
	fsys := fstest.MapFS{
		"main.go": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte(`package main
import (
	"embed"
	"fmt"
)
//go:embed a.txt
//go:embed b.txt
var f embed.FS
func main() {
	a, _ := f.ReadFile("a.txt")
	b, _ := f.ReadFile("b.txt")
	fmt.Println(string(a), string(b))
}
`)},
		"a.txt": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte("A")},
		"b.txt": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte("B")},
	}
	out, err := runEmbed(t, fsys, "")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if out != "A B\n" {
		t.Fatalf("got %q", out)
	}
}

func TestEmbedSubdirSource(t *testing.T) {
	fsys := fstest.MapFS{
		"pkg/main.go": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte(`package main
import (
	_ "embed"
	"fmt"
)
//go:embed data.txt
var s string
func main() { fmt.Println(s) }
`)},
		"pkg/data.txt": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte("subdir data")},
	}
	i := interp.New(interp.Options{SourcecodeFilesystem: fsys})
	if err := i.Use(stdlib.Symbols); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var stdout bytes.Buffer
	i.Stdout = &stdout
	_, err := i.EvalPath("pkg/main.go")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if stdout.String() != "subdir data\n" {
		t.Fatalf("got %q", stdout.String())
	}
}

func TestEmbedReadDirFile(t *testing.T) {
	fsys := fstest.MapFS{
		"main.go": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte(`package main
import (
	"embed"
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
)
//go:embed data
var f embed.FS
func main() {
	d, err := f.Open("data")
	if err != nil { fmt.Println("ERR", err); return }
	rd, ok := d.(fs.ReadDirFile)
	if !ok { fmt.Println("not ReadDirFile"); return }
	entries, err := rd.ReadDir(-1)
	if err != nil { fmt.Println("ERR2", err); return }
	fmt.Println(len(entries), entries[0].Name())
}
`)},
		"data/a.txt": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte("A")},
		"data/b.txt": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte("B")},
	}
	out, err := runEmbed(t, fsys, "")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if out != "2 a.txt\n" {
		t.Fatalf("got %q", out)
	}
}

func TestEmbedPatternTopLevelOnly(t *testing.T) {
	fsys := fstest.MapFS{
		"main.go": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte(`package main
import (
	"embed"
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
)
//go:embed *.txt
var f embed.FS
func main() {
	entries, _ := fs.ReadDir(f, ".")
	fmt.Println(len(entries))
	_, err := f.ReadFile("sub/x.txt")
	if err == nil { fmt.Println("ERR") } else { fmt.Println("not included") }
}
`)},
		"a.txt":      &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte("A")},
		"sub/x.txt":  &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte("X")},
	}
	out, err := runEmbed(t, fsys, "")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if out != "1\nnot included\n" {
		t.Fatalf("got %q", out)
	}
}

1431:func getFrame(f *frame, l int) *frame {
1433:	case globalFrame:
func getFrame(f *frame, l int) *frame {
	switch l {
	case globalFrame:
		return f.root
	case 0:
		return f
	case 1:
		return f.anc
	case 2:
		return f.anc.anc
	}
	for ; l > 0; l-- {
		f = f.anc
	}
	return f
}

END STEP

func TestEmbedImportedPackage(t *testing.T) {
	fsys := fstest.MapFS{
		"main.go": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte(`package main
import (
	"fmt"
	"./mypkg"
)
func main() { fmt.Println(mypkg.Get()) }
`)},
		"mypkg/mypkg.go": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte(`package mypkg
import _ "embed"
//go:embed data.txt
var S string
func Get() string { return S }
`)},
		"mypkg/data.txt": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte("from pkg")},
	}
	i := interp.New(interp.Options{SourcecodeFilesystem: fsys, GoPath: "."})
	if err := i.Use(stdlib.Symbols); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var stdout bytes.Buffer
	i.Stdout = &stdout
	_, err := i.EvalPath("main.go")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if stdout.String() != "from pkg\n" {
		t.Fatalf("got %q", stdout.String())
	}
}

interp/ast.go:344:func (interp *Interpreter) firstToken(src string) token.Token {
func (interp *Interpreter) firstToken(src string) token.Token {
	var s scanner.Scanner
	file := interp.fset.AddFile("", interp.fset.Base(), len(src))
	s.Init(file, []byte(src), nil, 0)

	_, tok, _ := s.Scan()
	return tok
}

func ignoreError(err error, src string) bool {
	se, ok := err.(scanner.ErrorList)
	if !ok {
		return false
	}
	if len(se) == 0 {
		return false
	}
	return ignoreScannerError(se[0], src)
}

func wrapInMain(src string) string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("package main; func main() {%s\n}", src)
}

func (interp *Interpreter) parse(src, name string, inc bool) (node ast.Node, err error) {
	mode := parser.DeclarationErrors | parser.ParseComments

	// Allow incremental parsing of declarations or statements, by inserting
	// them in a pseudo file package or function. Those statements or
	// declarations will be always evaluated in the global scope.
	var tok token.Token

STEP 140 TOOL 1

OBSERVATION:
returncode=0
package interp

import (
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"go/ast"
	"go/token"
	"io/fs"
	"path"

	"github.com/traefik/yaegi/internal/embedfs"
	"reflect"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
)

// embedData stores the resolved files of a //go:embed directive.
type embedData struct {
	dir      string
	patterns []string
	files    map[string][]byte
}

// embedPatterns extracts the //go:embed directives from a comment group.
// It returns the list of patterns found in all the embed lines.
func embedPatterns(cg *ast.CommentGroup) ([]string, error) {
	if cg == nil {
		return nil, nil
	}
	var patterns []string
	for _, c := range cg.List {
		text := c.Text
		if len(text) < len("//go:embed") || text[:len("//go:embed")] != "//go:embed" {
			continue
		}
		rest := text[len("//go:embed"):]
		if rest != "" && rest[0] != ' ' && rest[0] != '\t' {
			// Not a go:embed directive, just a comment starting with go:embed.
			continue
		}
		ps, err := parseEmbedPatterns(rest)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		patterns = append(patterns, ps...)
	}
	return patterns, nil
}

// parseEmbedPatterns splits a go:embed line into its patterns. Quoted
// patterns are supported as in the Go specification.
func parseEmbedPatterns(s string) ([]string, error) {
	s = strings.TrimSpace(s)
	if s == "" {
		return nil, errors.New("go:embed: empty pattern")
	}
	var patterns []string
	for len(s) > 0 {
		switch s[0] {
		case '"', '`':
			// Find the end of the quoted string.
			end := 1
			for end < len(s) {
				if s[0] == '"' && s[end] == '\\' {
					end += 2
					continue
				}
				if s[end] == s[0] {
					break
				}
				end++
			}
			if end >= len(s) {
				return nil, errors.New("go:embed: unterminated quoted pattern")
			}
			p, err := strconv.Unquote(s[:end+1])
			if err != nil {
				return nil, err
			}
			patterns = append(patterns, p)
			s = strings.TrimSpace(s[end+1:])
		default:
			end := strings.IndexAny(s, " \t\r\n")
			if end < 0 {
				end = len(s)
			}
			patterns = append(patterns, s[:end])
			s = strings.TrimSpace(s[end:])
		}
	}
	return patterns, nil
}

// splitAll returns the pattern without the "all:" prefix and a boolean
// indicating whether the prefix was present.
func splitAll(pattern string) (string, bool) {
	if strings.HasPrefix(pattern, "all:") {
		return pattern[4:], true
	}
	return pattern, false
}

// validateEmbedPattern checks that a pattern is valid for use in a
// //go:embed directive.
func validateEmbedPattern(pattern string) error {
	if pattern == "" {
		return errors.New("go:embed: empty pattern")
	}
	if pattern == "." || pattern == ".." {
		return fmt.Errorf("go:embed: invalid pattern %q", pattern)
	}
	if strings.HasPrefix(pattern, "/") || strings.HasSuffix(pattern, "/") {
		return fmt.Errorf("go:embed: invalid pattern %q", pattern)
	}
	if strings.Contains(pattern, "\\") {
		return fmt.Errorf("go:embed: invalid pattern %q", pattern)
	}
	for _, part := range strings.Split(pattern, "/") {
		if part == "" || part == "." || part == ".." {
			return fmt.Errorf("go:embed: invalid pattern %q", pattern)
		}
	}
	if _, err := path.Match(pattern, "dummy"); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("go:embed: invalid pattern %q: %v", pattern, err)
	}
	return nil
}

// visible reports whether a relative path contains no hidden component
// (a component beginning with '.' or '_').
func visible(name string) bool {
	for _, part := range strings.Split(name, "/") {
		if strings.HasPrefix(part, ".") || strings.HasPrefix(part, "_") {
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}

// collectEmbedEntries walks the directory dir and returns the relative paths
// of all directories and regular files it contains.
func collectEmbedEntries(fsys fs.FS, dir string) (dirs, files map[string]bool, err error) {
	dirs = map[string]bool{}
	files = map[string]bool{}
	var walk func(root, rel string) error
	walk = func(root, rel string) error {
		entries, err := fs.ReadDir(fsys, root)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		for _, e := range entries {
			childRel := e.Name()
			if rel != "" {
				childRel = path.Join(rel, e.Name())
			}
			childRoot := path.Join(root, e.Name())
			if e.IsDir() {
				dirs[childRel] = true
				if err := walk(childRoot, childRel); err != nil {
					return err
				}
			} else {
				files[childRel] = true
			}
		}
		return nil
	}
	if err := walk(dir, ""); err != nil {
		return nil, nil, err
	}
	return dirs, files, nil
}

// resolveEmbed resolves the patterns against the source directory dir and
// reads the matching files from the interpreter source filesystem.
func (interp *Interpreter) resolveEmbed(dir string, patterns []string) (*embedData, error) {
	dirs, files, err := collectEmbedEntries(interp.opt.filesystem, dir)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	data := &embedData{dir: dir, patterns: patterns, files: map[string][]byte{}}

	for _, raw := range patterns {
		pattern, all := splitAll(raw)
		if err := validateEmbedPattern(pattern); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		matched := false
		add := func(rel string) error {
			if _, ok := data.files[rel]; !ok {
				full := path.Join(dir, rel)
				b, err := fs.ReadFile(interp.opt.filesystem, full)
				if err != nil {
					return err
				}
				data.files[rel] = b
			}
			matched = true
			return nil
		}

		// Match directories: embed the whole subtree.
		for d := range dirs {
			ok, err := path.Match(pattern, d)
			if err != nil {
				return nil, err
			}
			if !ok {
				continue
			}
			prefix := d + "/"
			for f := range files {
				if f == d || strings.HasPrefix(f, prefix) {
					if all || visible(f) {
						if err := add(f); err != nil {
							return nil, err
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		// Match files.
		for f := range files {
			ok, err := path.Match(pattern, f)
			if err != nil {
				return nil, err
			}
			if !ok {
				continue
			}
			if all || visible(f) {
				if err := add(f); err != nil {
					return nil, err
				}
			}
		}

		if !matched {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("go:embed: pattern %q matched no files", raw)
		}
	}

	return data, nil
}

// genEmbedVar generates the initialization of a global variable declared with
// a //go:embed directive.
func genEmbedVar(n *node) {
	next := getExec(n.tnext)
	data := n.embed
	if data == nil {
		n.gen = nop
		n.gen(n)
		return
	}

	// The embedded declaration must declare a single variable.
	if len(n.child) < 2 || n.nleft != 1 {
		n.exec = func(f *frame) bltn {
			panic(n.cfgErrorf("go:embed cannot apply to var with multiple names"))
		}
		return
	}
	dest := n.child[0]
	index := dest.findex
	level := dest.level
	rt := n.typ.frameType()

	n.exec = func(f *frame) bltn {
		v, err := embedValue(data, rt)
		if err != nil {
			panic(n.cfgErrorf("go:embed: %v", err))
		}
		getFrame(f, level).data[index].Set(v)
		return next
	}
}

// embedValue builds the reflect.Value of the embedded content for the given
// target type.
func embedValue(data *embedData, rt reflect.Type) (reflect.Value, error) {
	switch rt.Kind() {
	case reflect.String:
		if len(data.files) != 1 {
			return reflect.Value{}, fmt.Errorf("pattern matches %d files, but must match exactly one file for a string", len(data.files))
		}
		for _, b := range data.files {
			return reflect.ValueOf(string(b)).Convert(rt), nil
		}
	case reflect.Slice:
		if rt.Elem().Kind() != reflect.Uint8 {
			return reflect.Value{}, fmt.Errorf("go:embed cannot apply to var of type %s", rt)
		}
		if len(data.files) != 1 {
			return reflect.Value{}, fmt.Errorf("pattern matches %d files, but must match exactly one file for a []byte", len(data.files))
		}
		for _, b := range data.files {
			return reflect.ValueOf(b).Convert(rt), nil
		}
	}

	if rt == reflect.TypeOf(embedfs.FS{}) {
		return reflect.ValueOf(embedfs.New(data.files)), nil
	}

	return reflect.Value{}, fmt.Errorf("go:embed cannot apply to var of type %s", rt)
}

// embedDirFor returns the directory of the source file associated with the
// given position.
func (interp *Interpreter) embedDirFor(pos token.Pos) string {
	filename := interp.fset.Position(pos).Filename
	if filename == "" {
		return "."
	}
	return path.Dir(filename)
}

END STEP

package interp_test

import (
	"bytes"
	"io/fs"
	"testing"
	"testing/fstest"

	"github.com/traefik/yaegi/interp"
	"github.com/traefik/yaegi/stdlib"
)

func runEmbed(t *testing.T, fsys fs.FS, src string) (string, error) {
	t.Helper()
	var stdout bytes.Buffer
	i := interp.New(interp.Options{SourcecodeFilesystem: fsys, Stdout: &stdout})
	if err := i.Use(stdlib.Symbols); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	_, err := i.EvalPath("main.go")
	return stdout.String(), err
}

func TestEmbedString(t *testing.T) {
	fsys := fstest.MapFS{
		"main.go": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte(`package main
import (
	_ "embed"
	"fmt"
)
//go:embed hello.txt
var s string
func main() { fmt.Println(s) }
`)},
		"hello.txt": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte("hello world")},
	}
	out, err := runEmbed(t, fsys, "")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

END STEP

/*
Package interp provides a complete Go interpreter.

For the Go language itself, refer to the official Go specification
https://golang.org/ref/spec.

# Importing packages

Packages can be imported in source or binary form, using the standard
Go import statement. In source form, packages are searched first in the
vendor directory, the preferred way to store source dependencies. If not
found in vendor, sources modules will be searched in GOPATH. Go modules
are not supported yet by yaegi.

Binary form packages are compiled and linked with the interpreter
executable, and exposed to scripts with the Use method. The extract
subcommand of yaegi can be used to generate package wrappers.

# Custom build tags

Custom build tags allow to control which files in imported source
packages are interpreted, in the same way as the "-tags" option of the
"go build" command. Setting a custom build tag spans globally for all
future imports of the session.

A build tag is a line comment that begins

	// yaegi:tags

that lists the build constraints to be satisfied by the further
imports of source packages.

For example the following custom build tag

	// yaegi:tags noasm

Will ensure that an import of a package will exclude files containing

	// +build !noasm

And include files containing

	// +build noasm
*/
package interp

// BUG(marc): Support for recursive types is incomplete.
// BUG(marc): Support of types implementing multiple interfaces is incomplete.

END STEP

func TestEmbedLocalVarError(t *testing.T) {
	fsys := fstest.MapFS{
		"main.go": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte(`package main
import _ "embed"
func main() {
	//go:embed a.txt
	var s string
	_ = s
}
`)},
		"a.txt": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte("A")},
	}
	_, err := runEmbed(t, fsys)
	if err == nil || !bytes.Contains([]byte(err.Error()), []byte("local variable")) {
		t.Fatalf("got err %v, want local variable error", err)
	}
}

func TestEmbedGroupMultipleError(t *testing.T) {
	fsys := fstest.MapFS{
		"main.go": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte(`package main
import _ "embed"
//go:embed a.txt
var (
	x string
	y string
)
func main() { _ = x; _ = y }
`)},
		"a.txt": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte("A")},
	}
	_, err := runEmbed(t, fsys)
	if err == nil || !bytes.Contains([]byte(err.Error()), []byte("group of variables")) {
		t.Fatalf("got err %v, want group error", err)
	}
}
EOF

STEP 157 TOOL 1
COMMAND:
cd /app && python3 - <<'PY'
p='internal/embedfs/embedfs.go'
s=open(p).read()

# Change FS struct and New
old='''// FS is a read-only in-memory filesystem built from //go:embed directives.
// It implements fs.FS, fs.ReadFileFS and fs.ReadDirFS.
type FS struct {
\tfiles map[string][]byte
}
'''
new='''// FS is a read-only in-memory filesystem built from //go:embed directives.
// It implements fs.FS, fs.ReadFileFS and fs.ReadDirFS.
type FS struct {
\tfiles *fileMap
}

// fileMap holds the contents of the filesystem. It is kept behind a pointer
// so that FS values remain comparable, like the standard embed.FS type.
type fileMap struct {
\tfiles map[string][]byte
}
'''
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new,1)

old='''func New(files map[string][]byte) FS {
\treturn FS{files: files}
}
'''
new='''func New(files map[string][]byte) FS {
\treturn FS{files: &fileMap{files: files}}
}
'''
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new,1)

# Update accesses to f.files -> f.files.files
s=s.replace('f.files == nil', 'f.files == nil || f.files.files == nil')
s=s.replace('len(f.files)', 'len(f.files.files)')
s=s.replace('f.files[name]', 'f.files.files[name]')
s=s.replace('for p := range f.files {', 'for p := range f.files.files {')
s=s.replace('for p := range f.files {', 'for p := range f.files.files {')
# readDir loop
s=s.replace('for p := range f.files {', 'for p := range f.files.files {')
# Open data copy
s=s.replace('append([]byte(nil), f.files[name]...)', 'append([]byte(nil), f.files.files[name]...)')
s=s.replace('append([]byte(nil), f.files.files[name]...)', 'append([]byte(nil), f.files.files[name]...)')  # no-op
# ReadFile copy
s=s.replace('return append([]byte(nil), f.files[name]...), nil', 'return append([]byte(nil), f.files.files[name]...), nil')
open(p,'w').write(s)
PY

returncode=0
// Package embedfs provides a read-only in-memory filesystem used to back
// //go:embed directives in the interpreter.
package embedfs

import (
	"errors"
	"io"
	"io/fs"
	"path"
	"sort"
	"strings"
	"time"
)

// FS is a read-only in-memory filesystem built from //go:embed directives.
// It implements fs.FS, fs.ReadFileFS and fs.ReadDirFS.
type FS struct {
	files *fileMap
}

// fileMap holds the contents of the filesystem. It is kept behind a pointer
// so that FS values remain comparable, like the standard embed.FS type.
type fileMap struct {
	files map[string][]byte
}

var (
	_ fs.FS         = (*FS)(nil)
	_ fs.ReadFileFS = (*FS)(nil)
	_ fs.ReadDirFS  = (*FS)(nil)
)

// New returns a read-only filesystem containing the given files.
// The keys of files are slash-separated paths relative to the filesystem root.
func New(files map[string][]byte) FS {
	return FS{files: &fileMap{files: files}}
}

// Open opens the named file or directory for reading.
func (f FS) Open(name string) (fs.File, error) {
	if name == "" {
		name = "."
	}
	if !fs.ValidPath(name) && name != "." {
		return nil, &fs.PathError{Op: "open", Path: name, Err: fs.ErrInvalid}
	}
	name = path.Clean(name)
	isDir, ok := f.lookup(name)
	if !ok {
		return nil, &fs.PathError{Op: "open", Path: name, Err: fs.ErrNotExist}
	}
	if isDir {
		return &embedDir{name: name, entries: f.readDir(name)}, nil
	}
	return &embedFile{name: name, data: append([]byte(nil), f.files.files[name]...)}, nil
}

// ReadFile reads the named file and returns its contents. A fresh copy is
// returned on every call.
func (f FS) ReadFile(name string) ([]byte, error) {
	if !fs.ValidPath(name) {
		return nil, &fs.PathError{Op: "read", Path: name, Err: fs.ErrInvalid}
	}
	name = path.Clean(name)
	isDir, ok := f.lookup(name)
	if !ok {
		return nil, &fs.PathError{Op: "read", Path: name, Err: fs.ErrNotExist}
	}
	if isDir {
		return nil, &fs.PathError{Op: "read", Path: name, Err: errors.New("is a directory")}
	}
	return append([]byte(nil), f.files.files[name]...), nil
}

// ReadDir reads the named directory and returns a list of directory entries
// sorted by filename.
func (f FS) ReadDir(name string) ([]fs.DirEntry, error) {
	if name == "" {
		name = "."
	}
	if !fs.ValidPath(name) && name != "." {
		return nil, &fs.PathError{Op: "read", Path: name, Err: fs.ErrInvalid}
	}
	name = path.Clean(name)
	isDir, ok := f.lookup(name)
	if !ok {
		return nil, &fs.PathError{Op: "read", Path: name, Err: fs.ErrNotExist}
	}
	if !isDir {
		return nil, &fs.PathError{Op: "read", Path: name, Err: errors.New("not a directory")}
	}
	return f.readDir(name), nil
}

func (f FS) lookup(name string) (isDir, ok bool) {
	if f.files == nil || f.files.files == nil {
		return false, false
	}
	name = strings.TrimSuffix(name, "/")
	if name == "." {
		return true, len(f.files.files) > 0
	}
	if _, ok := f.files.files[name]; ok {
		return false, true
	}
	prefix := name + "/"
	for p := range f.files.files {
		if strings.HasPrefix(p, prefix) {
			return true, true
		}
	}
	return false, false
}

func (f FS) readDir(name string) []fs.DirEntry {
	prefix := ""
	if name != "." {
		prefix = name + "/"
	}
	type entry struct {
		base  string
		isDir bool
	}
	m := map[string]entry{}
	for p := range f.files.files {
		if !strings.HasPrefix(p, prefix) {
			continue
		}
		rest := p[len(prefix):]
		if rest == "" {
			continue
		}
		base, sub, _ := strings.Cut(rest, "/")
		e, ok := m[base]
		if !ok {
			e = entry{base: base}
		}
		if sub != "" {
			e.isDir = true
		}
		m[base] = e
	}
	names := make([]string, 0, len(m))
	for base := range m {
		names = append(names, base)
	}
	sort.Strings(names)
	entries := make([]fs.DirEntry, len(names))
	for i, base := range names {
		e := m[base]
		entries[i] = &embedDirEntry{name: e.base, isDir: e.isDir, size: 0}
	}
	return entries
}

type embedFile struct {
	name   string
	data   []byte
	offset int64
}

func (f *embedFile) Close() error { return nil }

func (f *embedFile) Read(p []byte) (int, error) {
	if f.offset >= int64(len(f.data)) {
		return 0, io.EOF
	}
	n := copy(p, f.data[f.offset:])
	f.offset += int64(n)
	return n, nil
}

func (f *embedFile) Stat() (fs.FileInfo, error) {
	return &embedFileInfo{name: f.name, size: int64(len(f.data))}, nil
}

func (f *embedFile) Seek(offset int64, whence int) (int64, error) {
	switch whence {
	case io.SeekStart:
		// offset unchanged
	case io.SeekCurrent:
		offset += f.offset
	case io.SeekEnd:
		offset += int64(len(f.data))
	default:
		return 0, &fs.PathError{Op: "seek", Path: f.name, Err: fs.ErrInvalid}
	}
	if offset < 0 || offset > int64(len(f.data)) {
		return 0, &fs.PathError{Op: "seek", Path: f.name, Err: fs.ErrInvalid}
	}
	f.offset = offset
	return offset, nil
}

type embedDir struct {
	name    string
	entries []fs.DirEntry
	offset  int
}

func (d *embedDir) Close() error { return nil }

func (d *embedDir) Read([]byte) (int, error) {
	return 0, &fs.PathError{Op: "read", Path: d.name, Err: errors.New("is a directory")}
}

func (d *embedDir) Stat() (fs.FileInfo, error) {
	return &embedFileInfo{name: d.name, isDir: true}, nil
}

func (d *embedDir) ReadDir(n int) ([]fs.DirEntry, error) {
	if n <= 0 {
		d.offset = 0
		res := make([]fs.DirEntry, len(d.entries))
		copy(res, d.entries)
		return res, nil
	}
	if d.offset >= len(d.entries) {
		return nil, io.EOF
	}

END STEP

func runCfg(n *node, f *frame, funcNode, callNode *node) {
	var exec bltn
	defer func() {
		f.mutex.Lock()
		f.recovered = recover()
		for _, val := range f.deferred {
			val[0].Call(val[1:])
		}
		if f.recovered != nil {
			oNode := originalExecNode(n, exec)
			if oNode == nil {
				oNode = n
			}
			errorer, ok := f.recovered.(error)
			// in this specific case, the stdlib would/will suppress the panic, so we
			// suppress the logging here accordingly, to get a similar and consistent
			// behavior.
			if !ok || errorer.Error() != errAbortHandler.Error() {
				fmt.Fprintln(n.interp.stderr, oNode.cfgErrorf("panic: %s(...)", panicFunc(oNode.scope)))
			}
			f.mutex.Unlock()
			panic(f.recovered)
		}
		f.mutex.Unlock()
	}()

	dbg := n.interp.debugger
	if dbg == nil {
		for exec := n.exec; exec != nil && f.runid() == n.interp.runid(); {
			exec = exec(f)
		}

OBSERVATION:
returncode=0
		case *ast.GenDecl:
			var kind nkind
			switch a.Tok {
			case token.CONST:
				kind = constDecl
			case token.IMPORT:
				kind = importDecl
			case token.TYPE:
				kind = typeDecl
			case token.VAR:
				kind = varDecl
			}
			n := addChild(&root, anc, pos, kind, aNop)
			if a.Tok == token.VAR {
				var patterns []string
				patterns, err = embedPatterns(a.Doc)
				if err != nil {
					return false
				}
				if len(patterns) > 0 {
					if len(a.Specs) > 1 {
						err = astError(fmt.Errorf("go:embed cannot apply to a group of variables"))
						return false
					}
					n.embed = &embedData{patterns: patterns}
				}
			}
			st.push(n, nod)

		case *ast.GoStmt:
			st.push(addChild(&root, anc, pos, goStmt, aNop), nod)

		case *ast.Ident:
			n := addChild(&root, anc, pos, identExpr, aNop)
			n.ident = a.Name
			st.push(n, nod)
			if n.anc.kind == defineStmt && n.anc.anc.kind == constDecl && n.anc.nright == 0 {
===== ValueSpec =====

		case *ast.ValueSpec:
			kind := valueSpec
			act := aNop
			switch {
			case a.Values != nil:
				if len(a.Names) > 1 && len(a.Values) == 1 {
					if anc.node.kind == constDecl || anc.node.kind == varDecl {
						kind = defineXStmt
					} else {
						kind = assignXStmt
					}
					act = aAssignX
				} else {
					if anc.node.kind == constDecl || anc.node.kind == varDecl {
						kind = defineStmt
					} else {
						kind = assignStmt
					}
					act = aAssign
				}
			case anc.node.kind == constDecl:
				kind, act = defineStmt, aAssign
			case anc.node.kind == varDecl && anc.node.anc.kind != fileStmt:
				kind, act = defineStmt, aAssign
			}
			n := addChild(&root, anc, pos, kind, act)
			n.nleft = len(a.Names)
			n.nright = len(a.Values)
			if anc.node != nil && anc.node.kind == varDecl {
				var patterns []string
				patterns, err = embedPatterns(a.Doc)
				if err != nil {
					return false
				}
				if len(patterns) == 0 && anc.node.embed != nil && len(anc.node.embed.patterns) > 0 {
					patterns = anc.node.embed.patterns
					anc.node.embed.patterns = nil
				}
				if len(patterns) > 0 {
					if anc.node.anc.kind != fileStmt {
						err = astError(fmt.Errorf("go:embed cannot apply to a local variable"))
						return false
					}
					if len(a.Names) > 1 {
						err = astError(fmt.Errorf("go:embed cannot apply to var with multiple names"))
						return false
					}
					if a.Values != nil {
						err = astError(fmt.Errorf("go:embed cannot apply to var with initializer"))
						return false
					}
					dir := interp.embedDirFor(pos)
					var data *embedData
					data, err = interp.resolveEmbed(dir, patterns)
					if err != nil {
						err = astError(err)
						return false
					}
					n.embed = data
				}
			}
			st.push(n, nod)

		default:
			err = astError(fmt.Errorf("ast: %T not implemented, line %s", a, interp.fset.Position(pos)))
			return false
		}
		return true
	})

p='internal/embedfs/embedfs.go'
s=open(p).read()
old='''func (f FS) lookup(name string) (isDir, ok bool) {
\tif f.files == nil || f.files.files == nil {
\t\treturn false, false
\t}
\tname = strings.TrimSuffix(name, "/")
\tif name == "." {
\t\treturn true, len(f.files.files) > 0
\t}
'''
new='''func (f FS) lookup(name string) (isDir, ok bool) {
\tname = strings.TrimSuffix(name, "/")
\tif name == "." {
\t\t// The root directory always exists, even for a zero-value FS.
\t\treturn true, true
\t}
\tif f.files == nil || f.files.files == nil {
\t\treturn false, false
\t}
'''
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new,1)
open(p,'w').write(s)
PY

OBSERVATION:
returncode=0
	name    string
	entries []fs.DirEntry
	offset  int
}

func (d *embedDir) Close() error { return nil }

func (d *embedDir) Read([]byte) (int, error) {
	return 0, &fs.PathError{Op: "read", Path: d.name, Err: errors.New("is a directory")}
}

func (d *embedDir) Stat() (fs.FileInfo, error) {
	return &embedFileInfo{name: d.name, isDir: true}, nil
}

func (d *embedDir) ReadDir(n int) ([]fs.DirEntry, error) {
	if n <= 0 {
		d.offset = 0
		res := make([]fs.DirEntry, len(d.entries))
		copy(res, d.entries)
		return res, nil
	}
	if d.offset >= len(d.entries) {
		return nil, io.EOF
	}
	res := d.entries[d.offset:]
	if n < len(res) {
		res = res[:n]
	}
	d.offset += len(res)
	return res, nil
}

type embedDirEntry struct {
	name  string
	isDir bool
	size  int64
}

func (e *embedDirEntry) Name() string { return e.name }
func (e *embedDirEntry) IsDir() bool  { return e.isDir }
func (e *embedDirEntry) Type() fs.FileMode {
	if e.isDir {
		return fs.ModeDir
	}
	return 0
}
func (e *embedDirEntry) Info() (fs.FileInfo, error) {
	return &embedFileInfo{name: e.name, isDir: e.isDir, size: e.size}, nil
}

type embedFileInfo struct {
	name  string
	isDir bool
	size  int64
}

func (i *embedFileInfo) Name() string { return i.name }
func (i *embedFileInfo) Size() int64  { return i.size }
func (i *embedFileInfo) Mode() fs.FileMode {
	if i.isDir {
		return fs.ModeDir
	}
	return 0
}
func (i *embedFileInfo) ModTime() time.Time { return time.Time{} }
func (i *embedFileInfo) IsDir() bool        { return i.isDir }
func (i *embedFileInfo) Sys() interface{}   { return nil }
=== stdlib ===

STEP 205 TOOL 1
COMMAND:
cd /app && git commit -m "interp: support //go:embed directives

Implement //go:embed support for package-level variables of type string,
[]byte and embed.FS. Patterns are resolved relative to the source file
directory using the interpreter source filesystem, with path.Match glob
semantics, directory tree embedding, and hidden file exclusion unless the
all: prefix is used. Embedded values are initialized before the first
interpreted statement executes and are not overwritten by standard
variable initialization.

The embed.FS target is backed by a new internal read-only filesystem that
implements fs.FS, fs.ReadFileFS and fs.ReadDirFS, with sorted ReadDir
entries, fs.ReadDirFile directories and independent ReadFile copies. The
embed package is registered through stdlib.Symbols.

Add unit tests, an integration test in _test, and documentation." 2>&1 | tail -10

STEP 209 TOOL 1

cd /app && git show --stat HEAD | head -20
OBSERVATION:
returncode=0
commit 219eaa70d13328040750b017030fe02e0cecfc67
Author: Yaegi Dev <dev@yaegi.local>
Date:   Thu Aug 6 05:18:36 2026 +0000

    interp: support //go:embed directives
    
    Implement //go:embed support for package-level variables of type string,
    []byte and embed.FS. Patterns are resolved relative to the source file
    directory using the interpreter source filesystem, with path.Match glob
    semantics, directory tree embedding, and hidden file exclusion unless the
    all: prefix is used. Embedded values are initialized before the first
    interpreted statement executes and are not overwritten by standard
    variable initialization.
    
    The embed.FS target is backed by a new internal read-only filesystem that
    implements fs.FS, fs.ReadFileFS and fs.ReadDirFS, with sorted ReadDir
    entries, fs.ReadDirFile directories and independent ReadFile copies. The
    embed package is registered through stdlib.Symbols.
    
    Add unit tests, an integration test in _test, and documentation.

END STEP
